Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The hash operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 269. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 584 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the hash decomposition. By minimality, the element a^269 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 584-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
